Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The polymers variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 454 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 103 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the polymers space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 454 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 103. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the polymers object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 90. The moduli space has dimension at most 524 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 90 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the polymers structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 524. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
